How to read the data

Compare formats & numbered parallels

Compare the manufacturer’s published category odds side by side. Ratios retain their published units; cards per pack and packs per card are different measures. Numbering tables describe published specifications, not a guarantee that each player exists in every parallel.

Official sources & import notes

THE DETAILS MATTER

Official data.
Clear expectations.

“1 in 24 packs” means a card from that category, across all eligible subjects. It is not the chance of pulling a particular player. Serial numbering is a print run, not a pack hit rate.

Missing values are not estimates. Original manufacturer columns and notes remain available in card details. A listed subject is not guaranteed in every parallel. Imported odds categories alone do not establish an exhaustive parallel inventory.

All 16,799 rows from the official Panini CSV export are retained, including all original columns, literal card-set parallel names and SEQUENCE values. Empty numbering is not an estimate. Pack odds are absent from this export. Product year does not establish a release date.
